### 31.4 Persistence and LOCK-01 cross-check

PC-001..PC-081 are contiguous definitions with PC-061 retained solely as a retired tombstone; active persistence contracts total 80. Every record created/appended by CMD-001..CMD-136 exists in an active PC. Every mutable state field has one owner service. Cross-domain atomicity is command-owned. Orphan active contracts: 0. Double-owned aggregates: 0. Undefined state-transition owners: 0.

Every PC has exactly one mechanically checked lock disposition:

| Disposition | PC IDs | Normative rule |
| --- | --- | --- |
| GLOBAL_RANK | PC-002, PC-003, PC-005..008, PC-010, PC-011, PC-013..018, PC-020, PC-021, PC-023, PC-026, PC-027, PC-029, PC-031, PC-033, PC-036, PC-038, PC-040, PC-041, PC-043, PC-045, PC-046, PC-049, PC-051, PC-058..060, PC-062..066, PC-069..072, PC-076, PC-078, PC-079, PC-081 | Acquire at the exact §9.2 global rank/suborder; same type uses stable UUID. |
| PARENT_AFTER_PARENT | PC-022, PC-024, PC-030, PC-032, PC-037, PC-039, PC-052..056, PC-067, PC-073..075, PC-080 | Lock immediately after named parent: Prescription, Dispense, StockReceipt, StockCount, Invoice, Payment, TriageRecord, Encounter, Visit, ANCEnrolment/ANCVisit, or LabResult respectively; siblings stable-order. |
| INSERT_ONLY_OR_IMMUTABLE | PC-009, PC-012, PC-019, PC-025, PC-028, PC-034, PC-035, PC-042, PC-044, PC-047, PC-057, PC-068, PC-077 | Do not pretend-lock; acquire mutable parents/balances first, then insert under named source/sequence uniqueness. |
| QUERY_ONLY_TECHNICAL_OR_RETIRED | PC-001, PC-004, PC-048, PC-050, PC-061 | Organisation/authority are verified snapshots; PC-048 unique claim precedes domain locks; PC-050 uses isolated migration tooling; PC-061 has no table. |

The validation gate expands ranges, proves PC-001..081 appear exactly once, rejects a mutable command target in the last two categories, and constructs command lock-order edges to prove no inversion or cycle.

---

## 32. Complete Application-Command Catalogue

### 32.1 Normative common command contract CC-01

Every active CMD row is a mutation contract. CMD-107 is a retained retired tombstone and is not active. The named backend/application module is the outer transaction owner and calls core.services.tenant_atomic after server-side organisation/facility/actor/capability resolution, except rows explicitly labelled INTERNAL COMPOSED STEP: those rows name their parent CMD transaction owner, have no independent route, and inherit that parent's PC-048 claim. An outer command claims PC-048 before domain locks, acquires existing rows in LOCK-01 order and stable UUID order, invokes owner services in the listed order, writes all listed PC-047 audit facts, stores the safe result in PC-048, and commits once. Domain services own local guards/transitions only and never mutate another app.

Unless a row says NO, Idempotency is YES. Safe duplicate result is the stored authorized reference/current committed state. Stale input returns VERSION_CONFLICT with the minimum authorized current version. A concurrent loser returns ALREADY_EXISTS, ALREADY_COMPLETED, or VERSION_CONFLICT as Rank 1 specifies, with no replayed effect. Materially different reuse returns IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT. Any exception before commit rolls back idempotency claim, state, money, stock, and successful-action audit together; DENIAL-AUDIT-01 is the only separately durable exception and commits no domain state. Common canonical error families are NOT_FOUND-with-scope-hiding, PERMISSION_DENIED, PREREQUISITE_MISSING, INVALID_STATE, VERSION_CONFLICT, ALREADY_EXISTS/COMPLETED, and IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT; technical 5xx failures do not invent a Product-domain code. Common tests are service legal/illegal state, API/schema, permission, tenant isolation, success-audit rollback/denial-audit durability/payload, retry/fingerprint, stale write, and PostgreSQL single-winner where locks/constraints apply.

Fingerprint notation lists semantic inputs after canonical normalization; organisation, operation, actor authority revision, facility, and target IDs are always included. API names are stable logical operations; final URL spelling may be generated consistently without changing the operation identity.

Every row inherits applicable GSC-1..GSC-14 and the TIs named by its Rank trace; GSC-10 activates only after its Product-owned external-effect precondition is supplied. The scope resolved by CC-01 is part of every row's authoritative-record set. A row that names no financial effect has financial effect NONE; a row that names no stock effect has stock effect NONE. Created/appended records and audit events are exactly those stated in the owner/effects cell; unlisted cross-domain mutation is prohibited.

### 32.9 Command-catalogue closure

CMD-001..CMD-136 are a contiguous, non-reused register. CMD-097 and CMD-107 are permanent retired tombstones and the other 134 IDs are active design contracts. Every public mutating API operation above has one application transaction owner; every INTERNAL COMPOSED STEP names its parent transaction owner and has no independent route. Every row has one named owner-service sequence, an explicit or inherited idempotency fingerprint, exact LOCK-01-compatible locks, a success contract, atomic safe audit events, specific loser/replay/error behavior through CC-01, and PostgreSQL proof gates. Product-dependent seams are labelled IMPLEMENTATION AUTHORIZATION BLOCKED and are not route authorization.

Cross-check result for v1.0 FROZEN: 136/136 command IDs present; 134 active and 2 retired tombstones; 0 duplicate or missing command IDs; 0 high-impact commands without an idempotency owner; 0 mutating commands without a transaction owner; 0 lock-order inversions; 0 referenced persistence records outside PC-001..PC-081. These claims were mechanically rechecked before freeze.

## 33. Migration Decision Register

Migration is evidence transformation, not a license to infer Product history. Every migration below uses the same technical sequence: expand nullable structures and compatibility readers; inventory immutable legacy primary keys and counts; classify each row as deterministic or PC-050 exception; idempotently backfill using a recorded source key; verify relationships and monetary/state equations; enable new writes while legacy reads remain available; run two consecutive full parity scans with zero unresolved exceptions; validate constraints and FORCE RLS; then remove compatibility writes/readers only in a later deploy. A PC-050 row is operational evidence, not a new Product state and never makes an ambiguous row executable.

PC-040 therefore includes `print_count_exact:boolean:NN`: true for native receipts and exact migrated zero-count evidence; false when a legacy `receipt_printed_at` proves at least one print but not the full count. Native CMD-080 creates `print_count=0, print_count_exact=true`; CMD-081 increments the known count but does not turn an inexact legacy lower bound into exact history.

### 33.2 Executable gates, cutover, and rollback

For every row below, `L` is the immutable legacy inventory count, `D` deterministic target rows, and `E` unresolved PC-050 exceptions. Unless a row states a one-to-many relationship, the classifier invariant is `L = D + E`. Counts are recorded by organisation and facility as well as globally. A cutover gate is false if any SQL assertion returns a row, any `E > 0`, any Product blocker relevant to the enabled command remains unresolved, or either of two consecutive full scans differs.

Remove legacy no-Visit writer only after one release. BP-BL-03/04/09. |

Contract legacy statuses only after operational drain. BP-BL-04/09/10. |

Migration scripts must abort on count drift between inventory and backfill, write no PHI into PC-050 evidence, and be restartable under the same run/source key. Destructive down migrations, temporal-nearness grouping, and direct corrections to final clinical/financial/stock history are prohibited.

The CSV columns are exactly: `story_id, ac_id, release, owner_app, owner_service, application_command_id, state_machine_ids, cmc_ids, trust_invariant_ids, gsc_ids, api_operation, required_test_layers, postgres_gate_ids, product_blocker_ids, product_authority_status, blueprint_implementation_status, blueprint_section, implementation_slice_id, coverage_status, notes`.

The artifact is implementation evidence only. `product_authority_status` classifies the exact AC baseline: SUPPLIED has no named unresolved authority for that observable baseline; PARTIALLY_BLOCKED retains a supplied safe/default branch while a named branch stays inactive; BLOCKED means the affected AC cannot be implemented without the listed BP-BL authority. `blueprint_implementation_status` is FROZEN_BLUEPRINT_SLICE_AUTHORIZATION_REQUIRED for every row: Rank 2 HOW is active, but this freeze grants no blanket slice authorization. `N/A` is the only sentinel and is used only where a command, state machine, CMC or blocker is genuinely inapplicable. An AC text SHA-256 prefix in `notes` detects trace drift without copying PHI or changing Product authority.

### 35.2 Settled Blueprint-owned HOW

The following selected technical directions are active Rank 2 HOW under v1.0 FROZEN:

1. the fourteen Django apps remain the domain persistence owners; `application/` owns cross-domain commands and has no models;
2. the application command opens the single outer `tenant_atomic` transaction; domain services are transaction-neutral local-transition owners and do not call each other;
3. PC-048 stores only committed idempotency outcomes in the domain transaction; first-use ownership is database-unique and a failed transaction leaves no committed `IN_PROGRESS` tombstone;
4. LOCK-01 is the only multi-record lock order; same-type records use UUID order and new rows rely on parent/source uniqueness rather than pretend-locking absent rows;
5. Visit belongs to scheduling, QueueEntry belongs to scheduling, Encounter/clinical records to clinical, lab items/results to laboratory, Prescription/Dispense to pharmacy, stock ledger/projection to inventory, and all money aggregates to billing;
6. Payment has no authoritative Invoice FK; effective PC-039 allocations are authority, Invoice financial states are recomputed, whole-Payment reversal is distinct from CreditNote/Refund, and final value history is retained;
7. PC-028 is the stock authority and PC-027 a locked rebuildable projection; handover and stock deduction are one transaction; expired/controlled/uncertain stock cannot become usable through configuration;
8. migration ambiguity becomes PC-050 reconciliation evidence and blocks cutover; no state or episode is inferred from time proximity;
9. V1 operational time is Africa/Kampala and is not facility-configurable behavior;
10. CI Python 3.12 and Node 20 are the target major/minor baseline; exact patch/container digest pinning is an S-00 prerequisite;
11. internal safe audit writer/query seams are permitted for supplied TI-13 evidence, but no user audit route is mounted before AUD authority;
12. OD-T1 remains Product-owned OPEN: no outbox, broker, worker, event bus, or substitute delivery mechanism is selected.
